189b23ff260b2d35c1331ab233ec5e077a6506c5 [ diff ] 
ash: Remove GetActiveUserProfile() from CrosUsbDetector

Replace ProfileManager::GetActiveUserProfile() calls in CrosUsbDetector
with SessionManager::Get()->GetPrimarySession() and
BrowserContextHelper::Get()->GetBrowserContextByAccountId(), as GuestOS
VMs only run for the primary user session.

Start USB device detection when the primary user session starts via
SessionManagerObserver::OnUserSessionStarted() instead of on the login
screen in PostBrowserStart(), so OnDeviceChecked() always has the
primary user profile and checks persistent passthrough prefs for devices
connected before login.

Monetization runtime feature is enabled, giving pages a way to detect
native Web Monetization support.

[TabStrip] Add pinned tab drag-and-drop integration tests

Add cross-window pinned tab drag-and-drop integration tests to
TabDragDropTest and MultiTabDragDropTest covering:
- Dropping a pinned tab over a pinned tab (lands at target index in
  pinned section and remains pinned).
- Dropping a pinned tab (or multiple pinned tabs) over an unpinned tab
  (clamped to the end of the destination pinned section).
- Dropping an unpinned tab (or multiple unpinned tabs) over a pinned tab
  (clamped immediately after the destination pinned section).
- Dropping a mixed selection of pinned and unpinned tabs across windows
  (pinned tab clamped to pinned section, unpinned tab placed in unpinned
  section).

Bug: 493931798
Test: TabDragDropTest, MultiTabDragDropTest

0e4b9a9dbcb261dc8dc0ceb962ba2d4d459c337a
parent
5215ed18f7a82a3bfb58339df12f4dcd209576a8 [ diff ] 
Reland "media: Safe JNI ptr in MediaDrmStorageBridge"

This relands commit da9d4e061a6cc805cfed85a70056f39ec47faeb6
(https://crrev.com/c/8700296), which was reverted in
commit 986947eb4afc18471e51b7cb473f7c2f779f62bf
(https://crrev.com/c/8712223).

The original change only released the native MediaDrmStorageBridge
JniRawPtr in MediaDrmBridge.destroy(). When MediaDrmBridge.create()
returned null early (such as on emulators without Widevine support or
when L1 security level setup failed) or tore down via
MediaDrmBridge.release(), the JniRawPtr was left unreleased and
triggered LifetimeAssertException on garbage collection.

Ensure the JniRawPtr is always released by guarding
MediaDrmBridge.create() with a try/finally block for early creation
failures and releasing mStorage inside MediaDrmBridge.release().

Original change's description:
> media: Safe JNI ptr in MediaDrmStorageBridge
>
> Passing raw pointers between Java and C++ as long integer values is
> unsafe and bypasses type-checking.
>
> Migrate MediaDrmStorageBridge to use JniRawPtr to manage native
> references across the JNI boundary safely.

GestureNav: Use JniRawPtr in OverscrollSceneLayer

OverscrollSceneLayer stored its C++ peer as a raw long, bypassing JNI
Zero type safety and BackupRefPtr quarantine protection, and
OverscrollGlowCoordinator lacked a destroy() method to forward
teardown to its underlying OverscrollSceneLayer.

Migrate mNativePtr to JniRawPtr<NativeOverscrollSceneLayer> via
jni_zero::MakeRaw(), release it in destroy() before super.destroy(),
and wire OverscrollGlowCoordinator#destroy() into
ToolbarManager#destroy().

6f72c7c9faba5b316f903c7853760670b18b0240 [ diff ] 
Content: Use JniRawPtr in MediaSessionImpl

MediaSessionImpl stores its native ::content::MediaSessionAndroid
pointer in a raw Java long field. Passing untyped integer handles across
the JNI boundary bypasses compile-time type checking and lifetime
tracking, risking use-after-free bugs when the native object is
destroyed.

Declare a package-private NativeMediaSessionAndroid JniTypeToken mapped
to ::content::MediaSessionAndroid and store the borrowed reference in a
nullable JniRawPtr<NativeMediaSessionAndroid> created via
jni_zero::MakeRaw. Pass the reference to @NativeMethods calls via
JniPtr<NativeMediaSessionAndroid> and release and null out the handle on
native teardown so post-destroy getMediaImageBitmap() checks continue to
report null.

692cec58f288b8e8812972116461b525a1312d79
parent
35482cdc7eaf2d77c265bca2e256d6b2e93c75d2 [ diff ] 
[Session Restore] Fix cross-scheme session restore crashes and state inconsistencies

On tab session restore, `BrowserURLHandler` can rewrite navigation entry
URLs across scheme boundaries. Recent work in WebUI Bookmark support on
Android Desktop, for instance, can migrate pseudo-native
`chrome-native://` URLs to WebUI `chrome://` URLs. This triggered two
issues:

1. Stale `PageState` & Context Use-After-Free:
   `ContentSerializedNavigationBuilder::ToNavigationEntry()` applied the
   rewritten URL to the entry, but retained the deserialized `PageState`
   decoded from the old navigation, preserving frame state and origins
   from the previous scheme. Simply calling `SetPageState()` again on
   the entry clobbers the previous `FrameNavigationEntry` while
   `restore_context` still holds an unowned raw pointer to it, causing a
   use-after-free when subsequent entries share sequence numbers. It
   also loses the `temporary_referrer` or leaves
   `frame_entry->page_state()` out of sync on same-scheme rewrites.

2. Navigation API Origin Mismatch:
   When populating the Navigation API history entry list in
   `NavigationControllerImpl`, a `DCHECK` asserted that entry URLs could
   be derived from the pending origin. During cross-scheme restore
   transitions, this assumption fails and crashes the browser.
   Furthermore, `entry_arrays->previous_entry` lacked a similar origin
   check.

This CL resolves both issues.

Details for (1):
* content_serialized_navigation_builder.cc:
  `ContentSerializedNavigationBuilder::ToNavigationEntry()`:
  * Decode first into an isolated `temp_context` so intermediate
    throwaway `FrameNavigationEntry` instances do not pollute
    `restore_context` with dangling pointers.
  * On cross-scheme rewrites, synthesize a clean `PageState` via
    `blink::PageState::CreateFromURL()` and restore
    `temporary_referrer`.
  * On same-scheme rewrites, re-encode via `entry->GetPageState()` to
    keep the frame entry's page state in sync while preserving sequence
    numbers, scroll positions, and form data.
* `content_serialized_navigation_builder_unittest.cc`:
  * Add 1 unit test to `ContentSerializedNavigationBuilderRewriteTest`.

Details for (2):
* navigation_controller_impl.cc:
  * `ToNavigationApiHistoryEntry()`:
    * Validate that the entry URL can be derived from `pending_origin`,
      returning `nullptr` on mismatch.
  * `NavigationControllerImpl`:
    * `PopulateSingleNavigationApiHistoryEntryVector()`:
      * Break on `nullptr` to cleanly stop collecting entries at the
        origin boundary per the W3C Navigation API requirement for
        contiguous same-origin entries:
        https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/nav-history-apis.html#the-history-entry-list
    * `GetNavigationApiHistoryEntryVectors()`:
      * Pass `pending_origin` when populating
        `entry_arrays->previous_entry`.
* navigation_controller_impl_unittest.cc:
  * Add 1 unit test to `NavigationControllerTest`.

JavascriptTabModalDialog: Adopt JniRawPtr

JavascriptTabModalDialog stores its native
::javascript_dialogs::TabModalDialogViewAndroid pointer in raw long
field mNativeDialogPointer. Passing raw integer handles across the JNI
boundary bypasses type checking and lifetime validation, risking
use-after-free bugs when the C++ counterpart is destroyed.

Migrate mNativeDialogPointer to JniRawPtr with a package-private
JniTypeToken mapped to ::javascript_dialogs::TabModalDialogViewAndroid
via @JniType. Construct the borrowed reference via jni_zero::MakeRaw,
release it via JniRawPtr.release() on teardown, and pass JniPtr to
@NativeMethods calls.

[Bookmarks] Relocate CombinedBookmarksView to //chrome/browser/bookmarks

This CL moves `CombinedBookmarksView` and its unit tests from the
desktop-only `//chrome/browser/ui/bookmarks` directory to
`//chrome/browser/bookmarks`.

Why this is necessary:
* `CombinedBookmarksView` implements the `bookmarks_api::BookmarksView`
  interface (from `//components/browser_apis/bookmarks`), providing a
  unified view of local and account bookmark hierarchies with synthetic
  root node and UUID mapping.
* Although `CombinedBookmarksView` contains purely model-level logic and
  has zero UI dependencies (interacting solely with `BookmarkModel` and
  `ManagedBookmarkService`), it was originally placed in
  `//chrome/browser/ui/bookmarks`.
* `//chrome/browser/ui/bookmarks/BUILD.gn` contains an explicit
  `assert(!is_android)` guard, preventing Android targets from compiling
  or referencing any classes in that directory.
* Moving `CombinedBookmarksView` into `//chrome/browser/bookmarks` (the
  canonical cross-platform home for bookmark model utilities and
  factories) allows it to be shared with Android for the WebUI Bookmarks
  Mojo service implementation, while adhering strictly to Chromium's
  directory layering and platform boundary rules.

Changes:
* chrome/browser/bookmarks/BUILD.gn:
  * Add `combined_bookmarks_view.h` to `source_set("bookmarks")`.
  * Add `combined_bookmarks_view.cc` to `source_set("impl")`.
  * Add `combined_bookmarks_view_unittest.cc` to
    `source_set("unit_tests")`.
  * Add `//components/browser_apis/bookmarks:impl` dep.
* chrome/browser/ui/bookmarks/BUILD.gn:
  * Remove `combined_bookmarks_view.{h,cc}` and unit test.
* chrome/browser/ui/bookmarks/bookmarks_service_feature.cc:
  * Update `#include` path to point to `//chrome/browser/bookmarks/`.
* chrome/browser/bookmarks/combined_bookmarks_view.{h,cc}:
  * Update header guards and `#include` paths.
* chrome/browser/bookmarks/combined_bookmarks_view_unittest.cc:
  * Update `#include` paths.
  * Update test assertions using
    `bookmarks::TestBookmarkClient::IsDesktopFormFactorByDefault()` to
    account for desktop vs. mobile permanent folder visibility rules on
    Android builders (including in
    `AccountPermanentFoldersSignInAndSignOutEvents()`, which landed
    upstream while this CL was in flight).

No production code behavior change.

[DCSI] Add CopyImageAsync to SharedImageCopyManager

Add SharedImageCopyStrategy::CopyAsync() and
SharedImageCopyManager::CopyImageAsync() so callers such as
CompoundImageBacking::CopyToGpuMemoryBufferAsync() can perform
asynchronous GPU-to-SharedMemory readbacks via SharedImageCopyManager
without downcasting to SharedMemoryImageBacking:

- SharedImageCopyStrategy::CopyAsync() defaults to invoking Copy()
  synchronously and running the completion callback.
- CPUReadbackUploadCopyStrategy::CopyAsync() delegates non-SharedMemory
  to kSharedMemory copies directly to
  SharedImageBacking::ReadbackToMemoryAsync() into
  SharedMemoryImageBacking::pixmaps().
- SharedImageCopyManager::CopyImageAsync() dispatches to the first
  strategy supporting the backing pair, or invokes the callback with
  false if none matches.

[winhttp] Clean up ScopedWinHttpProxyInfo

- Move the members that allocate or free memory out of the header, into
  a new scoped_winttp_proxy_info.cc. Chromium style allows inline
  definitions and snake_case() names only for cheap accessors. The
  GlobalAlloc() and FreeString() helpers become functions local to the
  .cc file; GlobalAlloc() is renamed to AllocGlobalString(), so that it
  no longer shadows ::GlobalAlloc.
- Rename receive() to Receive(), and set_proxy() and set_proxy_bypass()
  to SetProxy() and SetProxyBypass(), since they are not simple
  accessors.
- Rename IsValid() to has_proxy(). It only checks whether a proxy server
  is set, so a "no proxy" result is not invalid.
- Remove proxy(), which has no callers.
- Document get() and the moved-from state.
- Pass the proxy info to SetProxyForRequest() by const reference.
- Add the header to the `public` list of the target, since the public
  headers proxy_configuration.h and network_fetcher.h include it.
- Test has_proxy() for a default object and for a bypass-only object.

No behavior change.

6822e03ef793346786f82b33c8d135d2293464a6
parent
b891bc8873a76a71404285877820723a40a6a2f3 [ diff ] 
[winhttp] Fix leaks in ScopedWinHttpProxyInfo

ScopedWinHttpProxyInfo could overwrite the proxy strings it owned
without freeing them, which leaks the GlobalAlloc-ed buffers, in three
places:
- operator=(ScopedWinHttpProxyInfo&&), which also cleared the strings
  of the object on self move-assignment.
- set_proxy() and set_proxy_bypass(), when a string was already set.
- receive(), when the structure already held strings.

No current caller does any of these: nothing move-assigns the class,
ProxyConfiguration::DoGetProxyForUrl() calls each setter once on a new
object, and it calls receive() again only after WinHttpGetProxyForUrl()
fails. The fix makes the class safe to reuse.

Free the owned strings before replacing them, and make self
move-assignment a no-op. The setters allocate the new string first and
keep the old one if the allocation fails. receive() now returns a
cleared structure. Use std::exchange in the move operations, which
also resets the access type of the moved-from object. State in the
class comment that the object owns the strings.

Add unit tests for the move operations, the setters, and receive().
The tests check the resulting state; no test can detect the leaks
themselves, since LeakSanitizer does not run on Windows.

Bug: None

2084ee03a917cf564708aa8857e2d09a3ead9b83 [ diff ] 
Content: Drop raw ptr in ContentUiEventHandler

ContentUiEventHandler is stored in WebContentsImpl's UserDataHost while
its C++ peer is owned by WebContentsViewAndroid without a native
teardown callback to Java. Caching a raw C++ pointer in Java risks stale
dereferences after WebContents destruction and would trigger
LifetimeAssert failures when WebView relies on garbage collection to
reclaim UserData entries.

Remove mNativeContentUiEventHandler from ContentUiEventHandler, guard
event forwarding on WebContents.isDestroyed(), and resolve the C++
handler from WebContentsViewAndroid on each JNI call.

55e3dda257c9b927c8ff45b21c2b5824d8956fba [ diff ] 
Feed: Own ReliabilityLoggingBridge via JniUniquePtr

Passing C++ pointers across the JNI boundary as raw long values bypasses
type safety and smart pointer protections, which risks use-after-free
bugs when native objects are deleted or accessed improperly.

Migrate mNativePtr in FeedReliabilityLoggingBridge to JniUniquePtr so
JNI Zero tracks the native peer lifetime and cleans up the native bridge
on Java destroy(). Return JniPtr from getNativePtr() and pass it
directly to FeedSurfaceRendererBridge to eliminate raw pointer casts.

Content: Key PageImpl map by Java peer ID

PageImpl has no native methods and previously stored a raw C++ PageProxy
pointer solely as a LongSparseArray key to retain the Java peer without
consuming JNI global reference table slots. Passing raw native addresses
to Java as map keys bypasses pointer safety checks unnecessarily.

Assign a monotonic peer ID in Java when PageProxy creates PageImpl and
store that opaque integer in PageProxy for lookups and teardown.
Detached PageImpl instances created by NavigationHandle test helpers
pass a zero peer ID instead of a dummy native pointer.

[Payments] Batch AndroidPaymentAppFinder and ManifestDownloader tests

Batch AndroidPaymentAppFinderTest and PaymentManifestDownloaderTest
with @Batch(Batch.PER_CLASS) and fastAutoResetCtaActivityRule().

These were unbatched because they appended --ip-address-space-overrides
for the test server's host:port in @Before. The network service parses
that switch only once per process, while each test gets a server on a
new random port, so every test after the first one in a batch hit
local network access blocking. Use "127.0.0.1:0=public" instead (port 0
matches any port) via @CommandLineFlags.Add, so the override covers all
test servers in the batch.

AndroidPaymentAppFinderTest also gives each finder its own
TestServerDownloader. A finder destroys its downloader when it
finishes, but its manifest verifier can still be downloading in the
background, e.g. to refresh manifests cached by earlier tests in the
batch. With one downloader per test, a second finder in the same test
could hit the destroyed downloader.

ExpandablePaymentHandlerTest stays unbatched:
testNavigateBackWithSystemBackButton fails most runs when batched.

Runtime of the 63 tests in these two classes:
- android-x64-rel: 4.62m (CQ baseline) / 5.24m (CI) -> 0.76m (-84%)
- android-desktop-x64-rel: 5.22m (CI baseline) -> 0.95m (-82%)

Cq-Include-Trybots: luci.chromium.try:android-desktop-x64-rel
Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chromium.android.desktop:android-desktop-x64-rel-15-tests|chrome_public_test_apk

b89d66224683d4e3d975639c6cf5e3ed4103cf71 [ diff ] 
[FSA] Match sensitive-path blocklist rules case-insensitively

This CL updates ChromeFileSystemAccessPermissionContext to evaluate
sensitive-path blocklist rules and home directory dotfile checks
case-insensitively.

Previously, while suffix blocklist rules compared path components
case-insensitively, regular blocklist rules and the home directory
dotfile check relied on case-sensitive path comparisons. When saving a
new file that does not yet exist on disk, path normalization cannot
canonicalize the on-disk casing, allowing case-variant paths such as
_VIMRC or VIMFILES to bypass sensitive-path checks on case-insensitive
filesystems.

Fixed: 581785712

Content: Use JniUniquePtr in WebContentsObserverProxy
📋 Metadata
commit
cc21b14b1d81085f02219ebd987f948a6a5364d5
[ log ]

tree
cf4afe01e4ead98e50a2dab541d19b94f86e6dd3
parent
79658068e8fb6fa76672c93330fad5fdd66ebe10 [ diff ] 
Content: Use JniUniquePtr in WebContentsObserverProxy

WebContentsObserverProxy stores its native
::content::WebContentsObserverProxy address in a raw Java long field.
Passing untyped integer handles across the JNI boundary bypasses
compile-time type checking and ownership enforcement, exposing callers
to use-after-free bugs if the peer is used after teardown.

Declare a package-private NativeWebContentsObserverProxy JniTypeToken
mapped to ::content::WebContentsObserverProxy and hold the Java-owned
peer in a JniUniquePtr<NativeWebContentsObserverProxy> allocated via
jni_zero::MakeUnique. Destroy the native peer via destroy().

Also clear Java WebContentsObservers in TabImpl.destroyWebContents when
slow shutdown defers native WebContents destruction, and stop
GlobalNightModeStateController before clearing UI_THEME_SETTING in
ChromeNightModeTestUtils teardown.

Bug: 40794873

Include unspecified IP addresses in implicit proxy bypass rules

Update ProxyHostMatchingRules::MatchesImplicitRules() to match
unspecified IP literals (0.0.0.0, [::], and IPv4-mapped
[::ffff:0.0.0.0]). Since these addresses route to loopback on POSIX
systems, requests to them should implicitly bypass proxy auto-config and
manual proxy settings, consistent with link-local and loopback rules.

🌺 Revert "[NTP Aurora V2] Use 16dp favicon background for MVT tiles in Aurora V2"

This reverts commit a5d8970e09f74899dd12478c3b1983d544e1aad7.
Culprit: a5d8970e09f7 - "[NTP Aurora V2] Use 16dp favicon background for MVT tiles in Aurora V2" (refs/heads/main@{#1738044}, https://crrev.com/c/8747981)

Reason for revert: consistently fails 5 RenderTests in
chrome_public_test_apk across android-16-x64-dbg-tests and
android-16-x64-rel.

Mechanism: Commit a5d8970e09f74899dd12478c3b1983d544e1aad7 (r1738044)
overrides SuggestionsTileView#onFinishInflate() to swap the MVT tile
icon background from a circle to a 16dp rounded square
(R.drawable.tile_view_icon_background_modern_rounded) when
NewTabPageUtils.isNtpAuroraV2Enabled() is true (which is enabled in
tests via fieldtrial_testing_config.json and ChromeFeatureList). Because
onFinishInflate() applies to all MVT tiles on the New Tab Page
(including the default "Add new" button tile on a fresh NTP), every Skia
Gold RenderTest that renders the New Tab Page directly
(FeedV2NewTabPageTest#testLoadFeedContent_Landscape) or captures New Tab
Page tab thumbnails in the Tab Switcher grid / Tab Grid Dialog
(TabSwitcherLayoutPTTest#testRenderGrid_3NativeTabs,
TabSwitcherLayoutPTTest#testRenderGrid_PinnedTabs, and
TabGridDialogTest#testRenderDialog_TabGroupColorChange) now fails Skia
Gold pixel comparison against the existing circular-tile goldens. Note
that the visual change itself is intentional for NtpAuroraV2; when
relanding, please triage the updated goldens for FeedV2NewTabPageTest,
TabSwitcherLayoutPTTest, and TabGridDialogTest in Skia Gold (or bump
their ChromeRenderTestRule revisions).

5 tests
(org.chromium.chrome.browser.tasks.tab_management.TabSwitcherLayoutPTTest#testRenderGrid_3NativeTabs,
org.chromium.chrome.browser.tasks.tab_management.TabSwitcherLayoutPTTest#testRenderGrid_PinnedTabs,
org.chromium.chrome.browser.tasks.tab_management.TabGridDialogTest#testRenderDialog_TabGroupColorChange__NightModeDisabled,
org.chromium.chrome.browser.tasks.tab_management.TabGridDialogTest#testRenderDialog_TabGroupColorChange__NightModeEnabled,
and
org.chromium.chrome.browser.feed.FeedV2NewTabPageTest#testLoadFeedContent_Landscape)
have failed in 6/6 recent builds across android-16-x64-dbg-tests (#9962,
#9963, #9964) and android-16-x64-rel (#7972, #7973, #7974), every
attempt in every one of them (plus 5 unrelated CQ CLs). This is not
flake: it reproduces on every run, so it blocks the CQ for every
unrelated CL that touches this suite. The regression appears at
refs/heads/main@{#1738044}.

Example failing runs:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-16-x64-dbg-tests/9964
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-16-x64-rel/7974

To run/deflake this test on CQ before relanding, add the following
footer to your CL description:
  Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chromium.android:android-16-x64-dbg-tests,chromium.android:android-16-x64-rel|chrome_public_test_apk

Reverting to green the tree first. Please re-land once the updated Skia
Gold baselines are triaged; this revert is about restoring CQ
throughput, not about the merit of the change.

Original change's description:
> [NTP Aurora V2] Use 16dp favicon background for MVT tiles in Aurora V2
>
> MVT tiles draw the favicon on a circle. The NTP Aurora V2 design uses a
> 16dp rounded square instead, matching the rounded surfaces around the
> tiles. When the flag is on, swap the icon background drawable; the
> circle is unchanged when it is off.
>
> This change applies to both the MODERN and CONDENSED tile layouts, i.e.
> phones as well as tablets and small-window configurations.
>
> Screenshots: https://docs.google.com/document/d/1bMRVyyqOd0-cAmyTbL9IhKNU9uSvDdpdCngSzSXIHnk/edit?tab=t.0#bookmark=id.pzxkqxesdcmy
>

[remoting] Limit Windows curtain-mode desktop resolution to 4K

8bc29e34b0f4106165b71a8abd050b777c803938
parent
ecbfb99b392c400f0e0e67db40a3474f457fa59d [ diff ] 
[remoting] Limit Windows curtain-mode desktop resolution to 4K

When connecting to a Windows host in curtain mode (RdpSession) from a
high-DPI display (such as a 5K or 6K Retina display at 2x scale),
UpdateSessionDisplaySettings() can fail and cause Windows' indirect
display driver (rdpidd.dll) to crash with protocol error 0x112f when the
requested virtual desktop dimensions exceed 4K.

Lower kMaxRdpScreenWidth and kMaxRdpScreenHeight from 8192x8192 to 4K
UHD (3840x2160 in landscape or 2160x3840 in portrait). Instead of
clamping width and height independently (which would distort the aspect
ratio), add ScaleSizeToFit() in remoting/base/util and update
remoting/host/win/rdp_client with GetBoundedRdpDesktopSize() and
GetBoundedRdpResolution() to scale dimensions down proportionally, clamp
to the 800x600 minimum, and align to even pixel boundaries. When
dimensions are scaled down to fit within 4K, client DPI is scaled down
by the same factor (with a 96-DPI minimum floor) to preserve the
effective logical desktop dimensions.

0b60f38cc701d77ff038fd3048e778512ff4b50b
parent
3c35d89945f800e3fb6fd5564a10dc9ffae137e9 [ diff ] 
[IndexedDB] Only share connections that match the renderer's target

With IndexedDBConnectionDeduplication, the browser previously shared a
connection whenever the client token had any open connection to the
database. However, the renderer's open request may no longer have a
connection to share (e.g. if the primary open request it piggybacked on
failed, or if the existing connection belongs to a dedicated worker
sharing the document's client token), triggering
`CHECK(shared_connection_target_)` in
`IDBOpenDBRequest::OnOpenDBSuccess` when the browser replies with a null
`IDBDatabase` remote.

Replace `bool request_shared_connection` in `IDBFactory.Open()` with
`int64? shared_connection_id` (the `transaction_id` of the open request
that created the connection the renderer expects to share). On the
browser side, record that `transaction_id` on the `Connection` once
`OpenSuccess()` is sent, and only share when a connection matches both
`shared_connection_id` and the full `BucketClientInfo`.

android: Pin Android 17 Desktop AVD creation config to emulator 37.1.11

Pin emulator_package in android_37_desktop_x64.textpb
back from 37.2.12 (Stable) to 37.1.11 (Stable), matching
https://crrev.com/c/8759211.

Emulator 37.2.12 suffers from host Gfxstream OpenGL ES memory leaks
(https://issuetracker.google.com/568480829) and an unlocked mWindows
teardown race during multi-process browser tests, causing exit=87
emulator hangs and disconnects on ci/android-desktop-17-x64-fyi-rel
(e.g. https://ci.chromium.org/b/8665854496225144145). Meanwhile,
emulator 37.2.13 crashes on ProcessCleanupThread in
EglContext::~EglContext() (https://crrev.com/c/8759211).

Emulator 37.1.11 predates both 37.2.x regressions while supporting
GLESDynamicVersion=on, Uwb=off, VirtioMouse=off, VirtioTablet=on, and
swangle_indirect.

[ log ]

Sat Nov 07 03:03:32 2026
tree
4c29d2f21c0bfd770258d925aa7a4d6187ddc45a
parent
3996e3c09aee28cb521b4de524b4725b45a586ef [ diff ] 
[task] Disallow sync work in RunOrPostTask while a TaskQueue has a fence

RunOrPostTask() runs its task synchronously when WorkTracker reports
that the sequence is idle. However, WorkTracker does not track tasks
blocked by a fence. As a result, RunOrPostTask() may run its task
synchronously ahead of tasks posted earlier that are blocked by a fence
(e.g. during content::RunAllPendingInMessageLoop(IO) in tests [1]).
This breaks sequencing.

This CL updates TaskQueueImpl to track whether a TaskQueue has a fence
(immediate or delayed) in cross-thread state. RunOrPostTask() does not
run tasks synchronously when the target TaskQueue has a fence.

[1] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:content/browser/scheduler/browser_task_queues.cc;l=297;drc=1eeed2603fc3184c35d058ef7cd148a8c04d38d9

tree
039224bdae0b9f9b6ab2b71ea487e1b81a0f7003
parent
a5c190a3be40d4f7aea2d6d49db85aafc47cbe67 [ diff ] 
Flush WebUI toolbar pipes in MoveMouseToElement for a11y tests

ForwardButtonAccessibilityTest clicks the forward button immediately
after WaitForWebContentsNavigation completes for the preceding back
navigation. When the WebUI toolbar is enabled (GetParam() == true),
ToolbarView::EnabledStateChangedForCommand(IDC_FORWARD, true) updates
the WebUI button asynchronously via PostPushToolbarState(), Mojo IPC,
and Lit rendering. Synthesized mouse clicks can reach
BackForwardButtonElement.onPointerdown_ while state.enabled is still
false, causing the click to be ignored and the subsequent
WaitForWebContentsNavigation step to time out with kSequenceTimedOut.

Call FlushWebUIToolbarPipes() in
ToolbarAccessibilityTest::MoveMouseToElement() before targeting a
TrackedElementWebUI so pending coalesced toolbar state pushes and Mojo
pipes settle before moving to and clicking WebUI toolbar buttons.

fc8ab5574aa08168521bf3a8defcc7b67359296e [ diff ] 
Clear SVG mask/clip-path caches when the last client is removed

SVG mask and clip-path resources have caches on their layout objects.
Before this patch, these caches stayed populated after the last
referencing client was removed, retaining unused paint records and
paths, and risking stale cached mask output if a nested mask's
display-lock state changed while unreferenced.

Clear the cached mask and clip-path data when the last client is
removed, matching gradients and patterns.

95968ddff4616cb6746e8de348c1a6299a3bcddd [ diff ] 
Optimize SVG resources in display-locked subtrees

This patch is a followup to https://crrev.com/1734940 and reduces the
cost of display-lock changes on pages with SVG elements:
* Avoid allocating and registering empty SVGTreeScopeResources during
  lookup-only queries (such as SVG geometry attribute updates).
* Skip notifying SVG resource maps when a childless non-SVG element
  outside <foreignObject> locks or unlocks, as it cannot be or contain
  an SVG resource target.
* Skip checking whether a LocalSVGResource's target is affected by a
  display-lock change when the resource has neither a layout container
  nor any clients to invalidate.

[TTC] Replace VoicePlateController with OverlayController

OverlayController owns all of the TTC UI shown on top of the browser
window and keeps it in the active window. Each piece of it is a TtcView
that draws itself from the session's UiState and talks to the session
through a TtcViewDelegate, which SessionViewImpl implements.

parent
4a63341c42c198f9b878223ce90ec8abd12e4a77 [ diff ] 
[crd host][win] Work around AppContainer slot exhaustion on Windows 11

On Windows 11 24H2/25H2, a kernel reference-counting bug in the
per-session LowBox number table (g_SessionLowboxArray, 'SeLs') leaks
entries whenever transient AppContainers (such as WinHTTP PAC worker
sandboxes in Session 0) are created and torn down. Once all 65,535
(0xFFFE) Session 0 LowBox slots are exhausted after multi-day uptime,
launching remoting_peer_connection.exe with AppContainer
SECURITY_CAPABILITIES fails in CreateProcessAsUserW() with
ERROR_NO_SYSTEM_RESOURCES (0x5AA) until the machine reboots.

Work around this issue in two ways:
1. Pre-warm the "chromoting.peer_connection" AppContainer when
   remoting_network.exe binds mojom::PeerSessionManager at startup via
   DaemonProcessWin::OnPeerSessionManagerBound().
   UnprivilegedProcessDelegate::PrewarmAppContainer() derives the
   deterministic package SID via
   DeriveAppContainerSidFromAppContainerName(), creates a restricted
   Untrusted-IL LocalService LowBox token without creating an on-disk
   profile, and keeps the handle open for the daemon's lifetime,
   reserving a Session 0 LowBox slot early after boot (and keeping its
   kernel reference count >= 1) only on hosts that enable the
   out-of-process PeerConnection worker.
2. Propagate ::GetLastError() out of LaunchProcessWithToken() and add
   LaunchWorkerProcessWithFallback() in UnprivilegedProcessDelegate. If
   CreateProcessAsUserW() fails specifically with
   ERROR_NO_SYSTEM_RESOURCES while AppContainer capabilities are set,
   reset app_container_ and retry once without SECURITY_CAPABILITIES so
   hosts upgraded in place on already-exhausted machines still connect
   using the restricted Untrusted-IL LocalService token and job object.

[Memory Coordinator] Don't notify consumers during teardown
📋 Metadata
commit
4a63341c42c198f9b878223ce90ec8abd12e4a77
[ log ]
author
Patrick Monette <pmonette@chromium.org>
Sat Nov 07 02:39:28 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Sat Nov 07 02:39:28 2026
tree
cea54c47a2e91761326411bf15f5f1be4faceb99
parent
863eb01161a55496f524f1cee14a1cc7c348d635 [ diff ] 
[Memory Coordinator] Don't notify consumers during teardown

When the BrowserMemoryCoordinatorImpl is destroyed, its policies are
removed, which restores the default limits and notifies the affected
consumers. There is no reason to do this: the process is shutting down,
and consumers have no use for a new limit.

It is also problematic. The coordinator is destroyed after the thread
pool has shut down. An async consumer's main thread half can still be
registered at that point, because its DeleteSoon() never ran, and it
posts the notification to the consumer's sequence. For a BLOCK_SHUTDOWN
sequence, that fails a DCHECK in TaskTracker::WillPostTask().

Add MemoryCoordinatorPolicyManager::PrepareForShutdown(), called at the
start of the coordinator's destructor. Afterwards, RemovePolicy() still
clears the policy's data but no longer notifies consumers, and any
other consumer update is a CHECK failure.

tree
2d875a6a4fda11626920e37a36a066881576a97b
parent
e867957482ed2d47050c8693743311c77f68ebfa [ diff ] 
Content: Migrate ContactsDialogHost to JniRawPtr

ContactsDialogHost stores its native ::content::ContactsProviderAndroid
pointer in a raw Java long field. Passing untyped integer handles across
the JNI boundary bypasses compile-time type checking and lifetime
tracking, risking use-after-free bugs when the native object is
destroyed.

Declare a package-private NativeContactsProviderAndroid JniTypeToken
mapped to ::content::ContactsProviderAndroid and store the borrowed
reference in a JniRawPtr<NativeContactsProviderAndroid> created via
jni_zero::MakeRaw. Pass the reference to @NativeMethods calls via
JniPtr<NativeContactsProviderAndroid> and release the handle on native
teardown.

tree
14ae9fd8c40ad15804bcc5e1ff68b481a72f76ce
parent
77743e5e8c4c8f406658f8ec7d8f8cc12bf0e821 [ diff ] 
[Autofill] Keep MandatoryReauth JNI bridge alive on failed show

On Android, the "Verify it's you to autofill payment methods?" bottom
sheet asks users after card or IBAN submission whether to require
device lock or biometrics on future payment autofills.

To show that sheet, MandatoryReauthBubbleControllerImpl passes a Java
bridge holding a raw pointer to itself into BottomSheetController. When
another sheet (like Touch-To-Fill) is still closing, Android queues the
opt-in sheet to appear next and returns false. Previously,
DoShowBubble() treated that false return as a failed show and dropped
its reference to the Java bridge early. If the tab then closed before
the queued sheet finished closing, the C++ destructor skipped clearing
that raw pointer, causing a UAF when the user tapped or closed the
leftover sheet.

Keep the Java bridge reference alive until
~MandatoryReauthBubbleControllerImpl() runs destroy() and zeroes the
raw pointer, matching Autofill payment bottom sheets. Note that this CL
only fixes the UAF. Because the controller is still owned by the tab,
interacting with a queued opt-in sheet after its originating tab closes
is now a safe no-op rather than running device authentication.

After: http://screencast/cast/NjI0MTY5NDk5NDM5OTIzMnxiOTczZWI2Ny04OQ

[Glic] Add passkey-gated opaque invoke error diagnostics callback

Surface internal invocation failure diagnostics to authorized internal
callers (such as ExperimentalTriggering, which needs live per-session
failure attribution on terminal TaskUpdate responses where client-side
UMA/Structured Metrics cannot be returned or joined per wire session)
without exposing Glic implementation types in Glic's public API:

- Format active task stage, initial client-load state, and client-load
  error reason inside GlicInvokeHandler into an opaque diagnostic string
  (e.g. "stage=...;load_state_at_start=...;load_error=...") that
  intentionally carries no stability guarantee.
- Gate SetOnErrorWithDiagnostics() / TakeOnErrorWithDiagnostics() behind
  InvokeErrorDiagnosticsPasskey in public/glic_passkeys.h, dispatching
  (GlicInvokeError, std::string) alongside on_error in
  GlicInstanceCoordinatorImpl::InvokeInternal() and
  GlicInvokeHandler::OnError().
- Note downstream non-UMA chrome_error_code usage on GlicInvokeError.

Design doc:
https://docs.google.com/document/d/1qqcCOq-g4rSIIUGRguIY9Tu8VpBVEazxBt2T8QsBpGc/edit

7ca7c723be1d97a37cf7e727ecc973de2148038e
parent
047239bfa302bf6d49a137c6945b22a492a22ddd [ diff ] 
gpu: Reject uncleared SharedImage import when EnsureClear fails

In GLES2DecoderPassthroughImpl::DoCreateAndTexStorage2DSharedImageINTERNAL,
PassthroughResources::SharedImageData::EnsureClear attempts to lazily
clear an uncleared SharedImage by attaching its texture to a temporary
FBO and clearing it.

Previously, if EnsureClear failed (for example, when importing an
uncleared GL_RGBA16F SharedImage into a WebGL2 context where
GL_EXT_color_buffer_float has not been enabled, or when BeginScopedAccess
fails or raises a GL error), DoCreateAndTexStorage2DSharedImageINTERNAL
still bound the uncleared service texture ID to the client texture ID.
Additionally, EnsureClear called glClear before checking
glCheckFramebufferStatusEXT, emitting a spurious
GL_INVALID_FRAMEBUFFER_OPERATION error, and called FlushErrors before
restoring temporary GL state and ending access.

This CL:
1. Checks glCheckFramebufferStatusEXT(GL_FRAMEBUFFER) ==
   GL_FRAMEBUFFER_COMPLETE before calling glClear in EnsureClear, and
   scopes the temporary FBO/state resets, ScopedAccess, and
   ScopedPixelLocalStorageInterrupt so they complete before FlushErrors.
2. In DoCreateAndTexStorage2DSharedImageINTERNAL, checks that
   shared_image_data.representation()->IsCleared() is true after
   SharedImageData construction; if not, creates a placeholder texture
   with DoGenTextures, inserts GL_INVALID_OPERATION, and does not map
   the uncleared SharedImage texture.

[User Ed] Enforce framework level flag state for sub-features

This change updates the Android User Education feature flag checks and
comments so that UserEducationFramework must be enabled for any of the
sub-feature flags to take effect. Because UserEducationFramework is the
top-level flag for the framework, individual sub-features like the
blue bubble and interruptive cooldown groups should remain inactive
whenever the main framework flag is disabled. We check the framework
flag alongside the sub-feature flags in the configuration provider and
promo controllers, assert it where a helper is only called after the
flag check, and add unit tests covering when the framework flag is
disabled.

Don't compute unused StartElement namespaces in the Rust XML parser
📋 Metadata
commit
1da70cfc430ad0d41b6f1c6d87e1401bb075127d

Sat Nov 07 02:05:33 2026
tree
0e62d4d8e7ffbf5f955059e77703c9090b19b60b
parent
6fd2c2d7e9b7b35613927dcc00301c6120bd24bd [ diff ] 
Don't compute unused StartElement namespaces in the Rust XML parser

For every StartElement event, the xml crate copies all namespace
mappings in scope into a new map (NamespaceStack::squash()), which
takes several allocations per element, but Blink never uses it. This
adds a start_element_namespace option to the crate's ParserConfig (as
patch 0004, upstream PR https://github.com/kornelski/xml-rs/pull/99,
not merged yet), which defaults to the current behavior, and turns it
off in Blink. services/data_decoder/xml, which uses the namespace, is
unchanged.

Results for xml-parser-svg-sprite.html in ms/run (is_official_build
without PGO, Linux x64, headless chrome; unmodified runner; 8
interleaved runs of 20 iterations each; median of the per-run medians).
A is libxml2, B is the Rust parser (XMLRustForNonXslt) before this CL,
and C is the Rust parser after this CL. Both tables use the default
fieldtrial_testing_config. For "study off", the
PartitionAllocMiracleObject study is excluded from the testing config.
Passing --enable-features=PartitionAllocSchedulerLoopQuarantine does
this, because the testing config skips any study that sets a feature
that is also set on the command line. That feature is enabled by
default, so its state doesn't change. Its default config still enables
the quarantine on the browser process's main thread and on the "amsc"
(sanitized object) branch in all processes, but not on the renderer
main thread. The perf bots also run without this study, since it is
marked disable_benchmarking.

PartitionAllocMiracleObject study on:

  | A: libxml2 | B: before | C: after |

tree
3078f0f6294aef5db6688dc8081b5f943dd2e40e
parent
40721b43e3ba844fe9ee311792ce00ba31766c86 [ diff ] 
Content: Use JniRawPtr in SpeechRecognitionImpl

SpeechRecognitionImpl stored its native peer as a raw long, bypassing
type safety and BackupRefPtr quarantine protection across JNI. In
addition, SpeechRecognizerImplAndroid is ref-counted across IO and UI
threads and can drop its final reference on BrowserThread::IO, whereas
SpeechRecognitionImpl and Android SpeechRecognizer must be torn down on
BrowserThread::UI.

Introduce SpeechRecognizerImplAndroid::UiPeer owned via
BrowserThread::DeleteOnUIThread so JNI callbacks dispatch through a
UI-thread peer that posts to SpeechRecognizerImplAndroid via WeakPtr.
Pass UiPeer to Java via jni_zero::MakeRaw(), take JniPtr<NativeUiPeer>
in Natives methods, and release the handle in terminate() and
onNativeDestroyed() when UiPeer is destroyed on BrowserThread::UI.

ba7b5f25757b9fc6915a24a355af3b99b458769a
parent
3d99c84b73c097d4ec144bd7a367e3dd6aeecd67 [ diff ] 
Skip converting ASCII input to UTF-8 in the Rust XML parser

XMLDocumentParserRs::AppendToSource() converted all input to UTF-8 with
String::Utf8() before appending it to the Rust parser's input buffer,
which first copies the whole input into a temporary std::string. ASCII
is valid UTF-8, so 8-bit strings that only contain ASCII can now be
appended as they are. This doesn't change the xml crate.

Results for xml-parser-svg-sprite.html in ms/run (is_official_build
without PGO, Linux x64, headless chrome; unmodified runner; 8
interleaved runs of 20 iterations each; median of the per-run medians).
A is libxml2, B is the Rust parser (XMLRustForNonXslt) before this CL,
and C is the Rust parser after this CL. Both tables use the default
fieldtrial_testing_config. For "study off", the
PartitionAllocMiracleObject study is excluded from the testing config.
Passing --enable-features=PartitionAllocSchedulerLoopQuarantine does
this, because the testing config skips any study that sets a feature
that is also set on the command line. That feature is enabled by
default, so its state doesn't change. Its default config still enables
the quarantine on the browser process's main thread and on the "amsc"
(sanitized object) branch in all processes, but not on the renderer
main thread. The perf bots also run without this study, since it is
marked disable_benchmarking.

PartitionAllocMiracleObject study on:

  | A: libxml2 | B: before | C: after |
  |------------|-----------|----------|
  |    11.9    |    17.5   |   14.5   |

PartitionAllocMiracleObject study off:

  | A: libxml2 | B: before | C: after |
  |------------|-----------|----------|
  |    11.4    |    16.6   |   14.1   |

Implements the base window and capture-exclusion scaffolding for
HostWindow::CreatePrivacyOverlay() on macOS:

- Adds PrivacyOverlayMac, PrivacyOverlayBackdropWindow, and
  PrivacyOverlayBackdropView.
- Creates one borderless, click-through (ignoresMouseEvents),
  capture-excluded (NSWindowSharingNone +
  CGSSetWindowCaptureExcludeShape) backdrop window per active display,
  failing closed and disconnecting the session if display enumeration
  or capture exclusion fails.

  background fill.
- Adds unit tests for window attributes, display resize/reuse/teardown,
  fail-closed error paths, and background painting.

b3662a7b506675fa8a68de5bbf9ea838cb617b90

b2b06b4497c3d408831bc703976a565eaa3869af [ diff ] 
DomDistiller: Hold weak delegate ref in C++

DistillablePageUtils previously returned a raw C++ observer pointer to
Java solely to index a static map of WeakReference delegates. Passing
raw pointers across JNI for map lookups exposes untyped native addresses
to Java and requires an explicit destruction callback to prune entries.

Store the delegate in JniDistillabilityObserverWrapper via
JavaObjectWeakGlobalRef and pass the resolved Java object directly to
callOnIsPageDistillableUpdate when still alive. This removes the raw
pointer return value, the Java lookup map, and the native teardown
callback while CheckedObserver continues to unregister itself on
destruction.

0c602aff620918105a3355ccf46c085524f0939d
parent
dd1bae63ec1d9a1820d596b98d6d462a1dc9cbc7 [ diff ] 
[WebUI Omnibox] Skip Mac activation focus restore on mouse press

On macOS, clicking into an unfocused Full WebUI omnibox popup that holds
an uncommitted draft causes AppKit to make the popup NSWindow key
synchronously before dispatching `mouseDown:` to Blink.

Because `OmniboxPopupFullPresenter::OnWidgetActivationChanged()` pushed
focus to the WebUI synchronously on popup window activation, the AIM
button (`#composeButton`) was revealed under the cursor before Blink
hit-tested the press, causing clicks near the right side of the omnibox
to activate AI Mode instead of focusing the input.

Guard the macOS activation focus restore with
`!widget->IsMouseButtonDown()` so clicks into the popup let Blink's
`mousedown` focus the input naturally while non-click reactivations
(such as Space swipes or Cmd+Tab) still restore omnibox focus.

Before: http://screencast/cast/NDc4MzI2NjIwMzY5NzE1MnxmYjNhMjIyZS1hMA
After: http://screencast/cast/NDk4NzE4OTYwNjc0NDA2NHxmMjI5ODMzNC0zNQ
Fixed: b:574637796

4c013d7b9d29e9f37ff199079a8e81aafb265683 [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Replace OmniboxDrawableState#isLarge with an IconSize class

Replaces the boolean OmniboxDrawableState#isLarge with an @IntDef
IconSize {SMALL, MEDIUM, LARGE} and adds size-class based accessors to
OmniboxResourceProvider (getEdgeSize(IconSize), getEdgeSizeDp(IconSize),
getIconRoundingRadius(IconSize)).

The new MEDIUM class maps to a 26dp edge (22dp on desktop form factors).
It is intended for icons whose glyphs carry more internal padding than
the Android XML drawables used today (e.g. natively rasterized
gfx::VectorIcons fill ~2/3 of their canvas vs ~0.72 for the XML icons),
so that they can be presented at the same optical size as SMALL icons.

No behavior change: all existing call sites map true -> LARGE and
false -> SMALL.

3402fcb4b822b20ba57809bcbcf6e69ca0ac1761 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Decrypt Remote Actor payloads off the UI thread

ChromeAppStartupParameters previously decrypted Remote Actor URL payloads synchronously while handling deep links on the UI thread, which reads from the iOS Keychain via base::ScopedBlockingCall and triggers a DCHECK crash when blocking is disallowed on the main thread.

Split URL parameter extraction from Keychain-backed payload decryption:
- Make RemoteActorRequest::request optional so URL parsing can capture the encrypted payload and nonce without accessing the Keychain.
- Add non-blocking ExtractRemoteActorRequestFromURL() and use it in +[ChromeAppStartupParameters startupParametersForRemoteActorWithAppID: completeURL: forceApplicationMode:].
- Defer ParseRemoteActorPayload() to a base::ThreadPool task with base::MayBlock() in SceneController when request is not yet populated, then resume bottom sheet presentation on the UI thread.

tree
111ab039b56f274417af88c74a4df697ad0ca8b1
parent
f633e8659a54b4530492cb53926757688ed2f757 [ diff ] 
webnn: Use GPU helper bundle ID for Core ML cache on macOS

The browser creates the Core ML E5RT cache directory using the outer
app's bundle ID plus ".helper". The GPU sandbox instead allows the cache
under the bundle ID of the helper app the GPU process runs in. Channel
customization (Canary/Dev/Beta) changes only the outer app's bundle ID,
so the two names differ. If the helper's cache parent directory does not
already exist, the sandbox prevents the GPU process from creating it.
Core ML then falls back to Espresso, and WebNN results can differ.

Create the cache directory using the bundle ID read from the default
helper app (ChildProcessHost::CHILD_NORMAL). SwiftShader is disabled by
default, so the GPU process runs in that helper. When SwiftShader is
enabled, the GPU process runs in the (GPU) helper, which has the same
bundle ID. Skip setup if the helper has no app bundle or its bundle ID
cannot be read. Add tests for a different helper bundle ID and a missing
bundle.

[Built-in AI] Count prefilled context in Writing Assistance token usage

Summarizer, Writer, and Rewriter prefill the prompt and sharedContext at
create() via SetInput(), but the create() input check, MeasureUsage(), and
the per-call input quota checks only counted the execution input.

Replace OnDeviceSession::GetContextSizeInTokens() with
GetInputSizeInTokens(), which returns the input context and execution
input token counts from one async call, and count their total in all
three checks.

[macOS PWA notifications] Add cross-path action histograms

This CL adds cross-path action/interaction histograms sliced by
{Style} (Banner, Alert, AppShim), specifically
Notifications.macOS.ActionReceived.{Style} in
ProcessMacNotificationResponse. This allows us to directly compare
click-through rate (CTR) and user close/button actions across Banner,
Alert, and AppShim.

(Notifications.NonPersistent.Shown / Clicked / ClosedByUser /
ClosedProgrammatically) for parity with persistent notifications.

[mac] DCHECK that unbounded surfaces aren't used with remote NSViews

This adds DCHECKs to the Mac entrypoints for the Unbounded Element API,
to make sure that PWAs (which use remote NSViews via the app shim)
never use unbounded surfaces.
RenderWidgetHostViewMac::CreateUnboundedSurface() now DCHECKs that
there is no remote NSView, and MigrateNSViewBridge() DCHECKs that there
is no unbounded surface when migrating to a remote NSView.

See this document for context:
https://docs.google.com/document/d/1uAxz9pk3twwkzamchxHTQiImIbKrc0cuKVDpzbn2wsQ/edit
(A Mac PWA Future We Can Support).

[WebNN] Remove in-renderer tensors on mojo disconnect

crrev.com/c/8438956 made WebNNTensorImpl::OnDisconnect() return early
for in-renderer tensors, relying on the fast path's Destroy() to remove
them. webnn_graph_impl_fuzzer only releases a tensor by dropping its
WebNNTensor remote, so remove the tensor on disconnect again with
posting DestroyTensorFromInRenderer() to the owning task runner, it is
fine if Blink's Destroy() also runs.

f11a27ef87f2b2fc57e75fb98cebd4f12622c613
parent
84d9f9503f4ddfd6257b4dc2ae458cc9184fb66c [ diff ] 
[Cardholder name] Offer the name update from PaymentsFormDataImporter

Some server cards were saved without a cardholder name, so Autofill
can't fill the name at checkout. When the user submits a form with
such a card, a valid name and a valid CVC, offer to save the entered
name to the card.

This CL adds an eligibility check to ProcessExtractedCreditCard() and
calls CreditCardSaveManager::AttemptToOfferCardholderNameUpdate() when
the matched masked server card is missing a name. The name update
takes precedence over CVC save. Virtual card enrollment still takes
precedence over the name update. If the name update can't be offered
(e.g. it is blocked by strikes), card and CVC save proceed as before.

It also passes the cardholder name extracted from the form to
TryMatchingExistingServerCard() before merging with local cards so a
matched server card does not inherit a duplicate local card's stored
name when the submitted form has no cardholder name field.

Metrics will be added in a follow-up CL.

Design: go/payments-autofill-cardholder-name-fix-design

Add move operations to copyable updater data types
📋 Metadata
commit
84d9f9503f4ddfd6257b4dc2ae458cc9184fb66c
[ log ]
author

Sat Nov 07 00:55:27 2026
tree
5a03c93126424fed109f71657cfd7fa64f30d0fb
parent
f3bc21a4f82111cb2af4bf925c155832e7cc5283 [ diff ] 
Add move operations to copyable updater data types

The following types declare a copy constructor but no move operations,
which suppresses the implicitly-declared move operations. As a result,
std::move() on these types copies them, and containers copy them when
they reallocate:

* update_client::CrxUpdateItem
* update_client::ProtocolParser::{Data, Operation, Pipeline, App,
  Results}
* updater::PersistedDataEvent::RegisteredApp
* updater::ProtocolParserXML::{App, Results}

Declare defaulted noexcept move operations for these types. For
ProtocolParserXML::App and ProtocolParserXML::Results, also declare the
copy assignment operator explicitly, since declaring the move
operations would otherwise delete the implicit one.

Moved-from objects are now left in a valid but unspecified state
instead of being unchanged.

Bug: None

[update_client] Add move operations to protocol request types

Updater, Data, and Ping declare a copy constructor, and UpdateCheck
declares a destructor. Either one suppresses the implicitly-declared
move operations, so moving these types, or moving App and Request,
which contain them, copies their strings instead. For example,
std::vector<Data> copies its elements when it reallocates.

Declare defaulted noexcept move operations for these types, and declare
the copy assignment operators explicitly so that the types remain
copy-assignable.

Bug: None

[Omnibox] Add OmniboxNativeVectorIcons feature flag

Introduces the OmniboxNativeVectorIcons feature (disabled by default)
that will control whether Omnibox suggestion icons are sourced from the
native gfx::VectorIcon definitions shared with desktop, or from Android
XML drawables as today.

This CL only adds the flag, its Java wrapper
(OmniboxFeatures#isNativeVectorIconsEnabled) and the chrome://flags
entry; no behavior changes.

parent
422b6d8865695b6a8f30ed65dedd3ea8254b1a4e [ diff ] 
Wait for Incognito window activation in AskBeforeHttp UI tests

The HttpsFirstModeIncognito and HttpsFirstModeIsolatedMode variants of
AskBeforeHttpDialogControllerUiTest open a second browser window in
SetUpOnMainThread() and run the test in it. On Wayland, window
activation is asynchronous, and on linux-arm64-wayland-rel-fyi the
compositor often hasn't activated the new window by the time the
Ask-before-HTTP dialog is shown. The dialog is then hosted by an
inactive window, so the "Go back" button doesn't get focus and the
HasFocus() checks (and the Esc accelerator) flake.

Wait for the Incognito window to become active after creating it, as
other interactive UI tests do.

Tested on linux-arm64-wayland-rel-fyi (Swarming, chromium:try):
before: 6 of 275 runs failed (CI binary of build 5057, repeat 5)
after: 0 of 1100 runs failed (repeat 20)

Cq-Include-Trybots: luci.chromium.try:linux-arm64-wayland-rel-fyi

16b2c85262f306c0036cd9de489939827441a0a1 [ diff ] 
Skip WindowLayoutInfoListener on non-foldable devices

WindowUtil.addWindowLayoutInfoListener() performs two synchronous
IDeviceStateManager binder transactions on the browser UI thread during
cold startup, even on non-foldable devices where FoldingFeature is never
present. Gate WindowLayoutInfoListener registration in
DevicePosturePlatformProviderAndroid on DeviceInfo.isFoldable() (on
Android 13 / Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU+), so non-foldable devices
skip registering the window-layout listener (intentionally excluding
isDesktop and legacy Android 9/10 foldables such as Galaxy Z Fold2 and
Z Flip that lack a standard Sensor.TYPE_HINGE_ANGLE sensor).

Validation: trace IDeviceStateManager with the fix 0/0/0 vs without
2/2/2; total 286/286/286 vs 293/292/293 (lever −2 of 2; total within
noise 1: a displacement under 1 calls is not detectable), k=3

Reduce V4L2 stateful instances to 2 for bcm2835

Pi4 H.264 decoder can only cope with a couple of instances at once
due both to being not very powerful and only having a limited pool
of VPU memory to use.

updater: pass ScopedServer providers a ProtocolVersion

ScopedServer response body providers received a bool that was true for
protocol 4.0 requests. Replace it with a ProtocolVersion enum (kV3 or
kV4), so call sites read as the protocol they handle instead of an
unnamed flag. Behavior is unchanged.

parent
6f229d62ff7adf3e9975528325ee2f35f9073ae5 [ diff ] 
[WebUI NTP on AL] Do not show Page Zoom IPH on WebUI NTP

TabbedRootUiCoordinator.initializeIph() guarded the cold-start Page Zoom
IPH ("not on NTP or CCT") with !tab.isNativePage(). With
UseWebUiNtpAndroid enabled (the default on Desktop Android), the initial
NTP (chrome://newtab/) is a WebUI page rather than a NativePage, so the
Page Zoom IPH bubble and its infinite MenuButton PulseDrawable highlight
triggered on cold start on large-form-factor devices. Under SwiftShader
on CI tablet emulators, the continuous PopupWindow and PulseDrawable
redraw saturated host CPU and the browser UI thread, inflating
ChromeDriver DevTools round trips and causing WPT timeouts.

Move the tab eligibility check into
PageZoomIphController.shouldShowForTab() and exclude NTP URLs with
UrlUtilities.isNtpUrl().

Bug: b:557344239
Test: PageZoomIphControllerTest

Mark move operations noexcept in updater-related code

Mark the move constructors and move assignment operators as noexcept in
chrome/updater, chrome/enterprise_companion,
chrome/browser/component_updater, components/component_updater,
components/update_client, and components/winhttp.

None of these operations throw in practice, since Chromium builds with
exceptions disabled. Some of the types contain members whose move
operations are not declared noexcept, such as base::Version and
mojo_base::ProtoWrapper; C++20 allows an explicitly-defaulted function
to declare a different exception specification than the implicit one.

For copyable types, a move constructor that is not noexcept causes
std::vector and base::flat_map to copy elements instead of moving them
when the container reallocates. Types such as CrxComponent,
ComponentRegistration, ComponentInfo, and AppArgs are stored in such
containers. For move-only types, the change keeps the code consistent
and helps type traits used by std::optional and std::variant.

Out-of-line defaulted definitions repeat the noexcept specifier, which
is required to match the declaration.

Also declare protected move operations on HistoryEventBuilder and delete
its copy operations. Its user-declared virtual destructor suppressed the
implicit move operations, so the defaulted move operations of the
derived event types copied the base members instead of moving them.

[WebNN] Add minimal validator for compiled EPContext models

Add a defense-in-depth check at the Compiler-to-GPU trust boundary of
the ORT backend. A compromised sandboxed Compiler could send a forged
compiled model that smuggles extra ONNX nodes into the GPU process.

EPs now declare a compiled_model_format in execution_providers_info.h:
kEpContext for OpenVINO and kFullGraph for WebGPU. A static_assert
rejects any enabled EP left at the kNone default, so enabling an EP
cannot skip validation by omission. For kEpContext EPs,
DispatchContextImplOrt::LoadCompiledGraph validates the model before
creating the ORT session.

Instead of full parsing or payload inspection, this patch adds
epcontext_model_validator.cc, a lightweight (~250 LOC) and EP-agnostic
implementation to enforce a strict structural check. It uses a minimal,
zero-allocation protobuf parser with base::SpanReader to merely ensure
the model is just a wrapper around the EP's precompiled blob:
- It must contain exactly one graph.
- The graph must contain exactly one node.
- That single node must be an EPContext (com.microsoft) node.

The opaque EP blob is left to the EP to validate. Rejecting other ONNX
fields that ORT acts on (e.g. initializers with external data) and
requiring embed_mode=1 are left as follow-ups, marked with TODOs.

The BigBuffer, which may be shared memory the Compiler can still write,
is first moved into private memory with MakePrivateBytes(), so
validation and session creation see the same bytes.

Every validation records its result to WebNN.ORT.EpContextModelResult.
On rejection, the GPU process reports a bad message and tears down the
whole WebNN context, so a compromised Compiler cannot keep retrying
through a reconnected compiler context.

Tests cover each validation result, and a fake ModelLoader verifies that
a rejected model reports a bad message, closes the pipe without
replying, and records the histogram.

The intended behavior of the validator is documented in
services/webnn/SECURITY.md.

Roll Arm AFDO from 159.0.8144.0_pre1735395_rc-r2-merged to 159.0.8147.0_pre1737478_rc-r1-merged

This CL may cause a small binary size increase, roughly proportional
to how long it's been since our last AFDO profile roll. For larger
increases (around or exceeding 100KB), please file go/crostc-bug.

Please note that, despite rolling to chrome/android, this profile is
used for both Linux and Android.

If this roll has caused a breakage, revert this CL and set the roller
to dry run mode using the controls here:
https://autoroll.skia.org/r/afdo-arm-chromium
Please CC c-compiler-chrome@google.com,gbiv@google.com,mobiletc-prebuild@google.com on the revert to ensure that a human
is aware of the problem.

To file a bug in Chromium Main: https://bugs.chromium.org/p/chromium/issues/entry

To report a problem with the AutoRoller itself, please file a bug:
https://issues.skia.org/issues/new?component=1389291&template=1850622

Documentation for the AutoRoller is here:
https://skia.googlesource.com/buildbot/+doc/main/autoroll/README.md
